# Denver Metro AI Concierge Prospects — Home Services
Research date: 2026-09-08/09. 7 prospects, mixed trades. Emails used are from company
websites or public listings only; Google review figures are given exactly as claimed by
the source, and labeled when not independently verifiable.

---

## 1. MH Electrical & Solar — Electrical / Solar
- **Website:** http://www.mhelectricalandsolar.com/
- **Phone:** (303) 915-0914
- **Email:** hello@mhelectricalandsolar.com (third-party public listing, findenergy; not shown on their website)
- **Address:** 2806 N. Speer Blvd, Denver, CO 80211
- **Decision-maker:** Matt Heenan — owner/founder, master electrician (named as owner in customer reviews on the company site)
- **Business:** Family-owned, founded 2015. Residential/commercial electrical (panels, wiring, lighting, EV charging) + solar installs and storage. Licensed, insured, bonded; in-house electricians.
- **Reviews:** EcoHome directory shows 5.0 stars from 48 reviews (third-party; Google rating not independently verified). Company site displays Google-rating and Solar-Reviews badges without legible figures.
- **Fit:** Owner-led shop quoting high-ticket panel upgrades and solar installs — one missed evening call can cost thousands. AI catches after-hours inquiries and revives stalled solar/panel quotes.

## 2. Sanders & Johnson Heating, Cooling & Plumbing — HVAC / Plumbing
- **Website:** https://www.sandersandjohnson.com
- **Phone:** (720) 619-2021
- **Email:** contactus@sandersandjohnson.com (public business listing, place123.net)
- **Address:** 7100 Broadway #7K, Denver, CO 80221
- **Decision-makers:** Jim Sanders (founder, owner/president) and Dan Johnson (owner); founded 1977
- **Business:** Family-owned since 1977, "one of the few remaining family-owned HVAC and plumbing companies in the Denver metro area." Heating, cooling, plumbing, water heaters, indoor air quality, same-day emergency service, financing. ~20 employees (Thumbtack). Hours roughly Mon–Fri 8–5, Sunday closed.
- **Reviews:** Company site structured data shows 5.0 from 50 reviews (source not independently confirmed as Google).
- **Fit:** Same-day/emergency demand with a closed-on-Sunday office = after-hours calls leaking to competitors. Replacement-equipment quotes need persistent follow-up — ideal AI use case.

## 3. To The T Plumbing, Heating & Air — Plumbing / HVAC
- **Website:** https://www.tothetplumbingandheating.com
- **Phone:** (720) 464-1609 (site also shows (720) 601-4468)
- **Email:** admin@tothetplumbingandheating.com (public listing, location.com business profile; also appears on a company estimate PDF)
- **Address:** 12487 Syracuse St, Thornton, CO 80602 (office: 1343 W 121st Ave, Westminster, CO 80234)
- **Decision-maker:** Nate — founder (first name only; wife Megan also listed as co-founder on PlumbersUp)
- **Business:** Family-owned and operated, serving since 2005 per their site (one listing says established 2018). Plumbing + HVAC, 24/7 emergency service, online booking, club memberships, financing. Team page lists office manager, marketing manager, dispatch coordinator, call-center rep.
- **Reviews:** Site claims "A+ BBB rated & 4.9★ Google reviews" (count not shown).
- **Fit:** 24/7 emergency positioning with a small dispatch team — peak surges (freeze events, heat waves) mean missed calls. Strong quote-follow-up angle on replacement estimates.

## 4. Green Mountain Landscaping (Green Mountain Enterprise) — Landscaping
- **Website:** https://greenmountainlandscapers.com/
- **Phone:** (303) 901-2215
- **Email:** Not published on the website — contact via website contact form (phone confirmed on site)
- **Address:** 11244 W 67th Pl, Arvada, CO 80004 (public listing)
- **Decision-maker:** Denis McCausland — founder, 30+ years landscaping on the Front Range, B.S. Horticulture (Ohio State); directly oversees all installations
- **Business:** Owner-operated design/build firm. Landscape design + installation, refreshes, retaining walls, sod, drainage, plantings, sprinklers. Typical project $2,000–$30,000. ~28 years in business (Porch).
- **Reviews:** Porch shows ~4.7 average from 40 online reviews (aggregated third-party; Google rating not independently verified).
- **Fit:** $2k–$30k projects start with a design consultation — while Denis is on job sites, evening inquiries slip away. Consult-booking + estimate follow-up are the natural AI wins.

## 5. ARCM Roofing (ARCM Roofing Inc.) — Roofing
- **Website:** https://arcmroofers.com/
- **Phone:** (303) 306-8334
- **Email:** Not published on the website — contact form at https://arcmroofers.com/contact (responds within 24h)
- **Address:** 5306 S Bannock St, Suite 218, Littleton, CO 80120
- **Decision-maker:** Anthony Hong — owner (Yelp business-owner info lists "Anthony H."; BuildZoom: "Owned by: Anthony Hong")
- **Business:** Family-owned, founded 2009. Residential + commercial roofing, storm/hail damage, drone inspections, insurance-claim assistance. GAF Certified Plus, Owens Corning Preferred, Google Guaranteed, A+ BBB. ~13 employees, ~$1.5M revenue (datanyze). Hours M–F 8–5; emergency roofing 24/7.
- **Reviews:** Company site's Google Reviews section shows 4.9 (142 reviews); company marketing claims 4.9★ from 500+ Google reviews (not independently verified).
- **Fit:** Storm-driven call spikes overwhelm a small office; insurance-claim leads need fast response and weeks of follow-up. Classic missed-call economics.

- **Reviews:** Site claims 4.9 stars from 1,000+ verified reviews (platform not specified).

---

## Notes / caveats
- No emails were invented. Where no public email exists (Green Mountain, ARCM, Peak to Peak, Just In Time), the draft file notes the website contact form instead.
- Google review counts are reported only as the company's own claims or third-party directory figures; none were independently verified on Google.
- Owner first names only (Nate at To The T; Anthony H. at ARCM) are used as published.
- Larger firms (Plumbline Services ~170 employees, Bell Plumbing since 1926) were researched but excluded as less aligned with a small-business target.

## Round 2 — 2026-09-08/09. 6 new prospects (none overlap Round 1). Emails used are from company websites or public listings only; review figures are the company's own claims or third-party directory figures, labeled as such.

### 1. Steele Bros Heating, Inc. — HVAC
- **Website:** https://steelebrosheating.com/
- **Phone:** (303) 347-1958
- **Email:** Not published on the website — contact via website contact form

- **Size:** ~33 employees (third-party directory estimate, not independently verified)
- **Reviews:** 5.0 from 12 reviews (third-party listing; Google rating not independently verified)
- **Fit:** Three-decade family shop with emergency service and replacement-equipment sales — after-hours no-heat calls and quiet replacement quotes are the classic leak. Owner-led, so less office drag is a direct win.

### 2. Allen Service — Plumbing / HVAC / Electrical
- **Website:** http://www.allenservice.com
- **Phone:** (970) 431-6574
- **Email:** Not published on the website — contact via website contact form
- **Decision-makers:** Jeff Allen and Mike Allen — third-generation brothers leading the company

- **Reviews:** "Over 4000 5-Star Reviews" (company claim, platform not specified)
- **Fit:** Large multi-trade family operation with relentless inbound volume. 24/7 positioning means constant phone duty pulling techs off jobs — exactly the interruption problem PING solves, plus heavy quote follow-up potential.

- **Reviews:** No aggregate rating found in public listings reviewed
- **Fit:** Pest work is seasonal and urgent — homeowners call fast and call around, so speed of answer decides who wins. Free inspections and recurring treatments both need fast booking and steady follow-up, with zero office staff to spare.

### Round 2 notes / caveats

# Round 3a — Plumbing & HVAC Prospects (2026-09-08)
8 new Denver-metro plumbing/HVAC businesses. None overlap Round 1 or Round 2. Emails used are from company websites or public listings only; review figures are company claims or third-party directory figures, labeled as such. No emails invented.

## 1. Blue Sky Plumbing, Heating, Cooling & Electric — Plumbing / HVAC / Electrical
- **Website:** https://www.blueskyplumbing.com/
- **Phone:** (303) 407-7961
- **Email:** Not published — contact via website contact form
- **Address:** 2055 S Oneida St Ste 296, Denver, CO 80224 (also Wheat Ridge, Evergreen, Longmont locations)
- **Decision-makers:** Jesse Lanyon (vice president, 4th generation) and Stephen Lanyon (president)
- **Business:** Family-owned and operated for four generations, roots back to 1916 when Archie Lanyon became a registered Colorado Master Plumber. Full-service plumbing, heating, AC, and electrical. Open 24 hours, 7 days.

# Round 3b — Electrical / Roofing / Garage Doors — 2026-09-08

8 new Denver-metro prospects, none overlap Rounds 1 or 2. Nothing sent, no forms submitted. All contact routes are website contact forms unless noted.

## Electrical

### 1. Over-All Electric — Englewood, CO
- Website: https://overallelectricllc.com/ — contact route: homeowner quote form ("Allie or Jake calls you back by the next business day"). Note: this is a customer quote form, not a general B2B contact form.
- Decision-maker: not named on site; quote callbacks handled by Allie or Jake.
- Profile: family-owned since 1981 (company claim), CO Electrical Contractor Lic. #1912, Englewood. Rating 5.0 on Angi (third-party). BuildZoom score 110, top 4% of CO contractors, 380 permitted projects in 36 months (third-party).
- Services: panel upgrades, EV chargers, lighting/fans/remodels, smart home/audio/security for homeowners; multifamily production, commercial, fire alarm design/install for builders.
- Fit: production crews on apartment blocks all day plus a service truck for homeowners, so phones ring while everyone is working. Strong fit for after-hours capture and callback triage.

- Owner names for Five Sons (Chris), Colorado Roofing Company (Anthony/Tony), and Elite (Randy Brothers) come from review quotes or third-party directories, not confirmed ownership pages. Light verify before deeper personalization.
- Over-All Electric and Five Sons only offer customer quote/estimate forms, not general contact forms; submitting B2B outreach through them would pose as a customer, same issue as ARCM in Round 1. Flagged in each draft header.
- Review figures are company claims or third-party listings, labeled as such; none independently verified on Google.
- No emails were sent and no forms were submitted.
